## 2026-10-06 · Grind ollie intent and deliberate rail catches

- Brief: distinguish an ollie/spin back onto the rail from a side exit or rail-to-rail transfer by direction timing. Follow-up: require another Grind press for the landing; missing it must bail.
- Elapsed prompt-to-playable: approximately **14 minutes** through native keyboard lite/full gameplay verification; isolated release and deployment verification followed.
- Sample the stick before Jump release. A neutral pop keeps the rail tangent and forward speed; subsequent left/right rotates without steering. Pre-held lateral direction launches at up to **4.8 m/s**, with a **0.3** intent deadzone, analog strength and **80–100%** charge scaling. Measured same-height displacement is **2.11/3.28 m** for tap/full campaign pops and **2.24/3.52 m** in parks. The launch direction does not also rotate the rider; release/repress or change direction to spin. Carry side momentum through grabs, rail catches and ground roll-outs. Retire R2/grab trajectory overrides; preserve authored vertical pop, gravity, ordinary board/on-foot air controls and balance difficulty.
- A fresh airborne Grind edge arms the next catch, including an early press held through cooldown. The original held button does not count on either rail. Same-rail catches require descent through the rail contact envelope; unarmed landings use the existing rail bail. Endpoint run-offs retain their anti-recatch latch, and catches/landings/respawns clear the ollie state.
- Validation: **68 scenarios / 4,516 actual controller frames**; neutral 180s, both rail directions, angled/sloping screen frames, analog/noise, tap/full charge, same-frame versus pre-held input, side exits, adjacent rails, fresh/stale/released Grind, modifier independence, roll-out, respawn and endpoints. Existing flip-chord, bare-spin scoring, balance re-entry and all **51 coping drop-in cases** pass. **24 native keyboard cases** in real Chrome cover campaign/park and lite/full rendering with no console errors. Production type-check and Vite bundle pass on an isolated latest-main checkout. No level data changes or full-suite run.
